# Title: AdBlock DNSMasq servers Lite
# Description: 适用于 DNSMasq servers 的去广告合并规则，每 12 小时更新一次。规则源：AdGuard Base filter、AdGuard Chinese filter、AdGuard Mobile Ads filter、AdGuard DNS filter、AdRules DNS List、CJX's Annoyance List、EasyList、EasyList China、EasyPrivacy、xinggsf mv、jiekouAD、AWAvenue Ads Rule、DNS-Blocklists Light、Hblock、OISD Basic、StevenBlack hosts、Pollock hosts、anti-AD、Phishing URL Blocklist、Malicious URL Blocklist、Online Malicious URL Blocklist、PeterLowe、SmartTV、HageziMultiPro、HaGeZi's Apple Tracker Blocklist、HaGeZi's Badware Hoster Blocklist、HaGeZi's Windows/Office Tracker Blocklist、HaGeZi's Xiaomi Tracker Blocklist、HaGeZi's Vivo Tracker Blocklist、HaGeZi's Samsung Tracker Blocklist、HaGeZi's OPPO & Realme Tracker Blocklist、HaGeZi's Gambling Blocklist、HaGeZi's Threat Intelligence Feeds、uBlock Badware risks、OISD Big、Stalkerware、Scam Blocklist、uBlock Ads、uBlock Privacy、AdGuard Tracking Protection、AdGuard Annoyances、lingeringsound adblock_auto。Lite 版仅针对国内域名拦截。
